# State sweep register

Every file in the codebase was read line by line: 2575 files in 14 shards, base commit bc5f50ef2 (2026-11-09).

1949 entries. S1 5, S2 61, S3 500, S4 592, IS 791. Kinds: crash 7, bug 1138, implicit-state 804.

The per-shard JSONL files beside this one are the source; this file is generated from them. "plausible" entries need a check against the code before fixing.

## S1: data loss, wrong send, privacy, crash or freeze (5)

### 01-044: read_zip accumulates one inflated copy per central-directory entry; a crafted theme zip exhausts memory

`src/app_theme/file.rs:341` · crash · confirmed

- Trigger: Import (or open) a .tdesktop-theme under 5 MB whose central directory lists ~65535 entries that all point at one local header holding a 4 MB deflate stream (about 4 KB compressed, size field 4 MB). Each entry passes the per-entry size check and is inflated and pushed into `out`.
- Effect: Memory grows by about 4 MB per entry (hundreds of GB total) until the allocator aborts: the whole app crashes (import runs on a background thread, but an OOM abort kills the process; store::load on the UI thread freezes it first).
- Fix: Keep a running total of inflated bytes (cap it near BACKGROUND_LIMIT + SCHEME_LIMIT), reject duplicate local-header offsets, and only inflate the entries ThemeFile::parse asks for by name.

### 04-052: enable() rolls back wrapped files after some OS-store keys were already deleted, losing database keys

`src/passcode/mod.rs:494` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Two or more accounts signed in. Settings > Passcode > Turn on. Every account's key is wrapped and written, the config is saved, then the loop `for account in &written { inner.delete(account)? }` runs. The first account's key is deleted from the OS secret store, the delete for the second fails (keychain locked/denied, secret service error).
- Effect: `rollback(&written)` removes every wrapped file and passcode.json, but the first account's key is already gone from the OS store and its wrapped copy is now deleted too. Its TDLib database can never be opened again: the account is lost (forced re-login, local data unreadable). Contradicts the doc comment 'no database key is ever lost'.
- Fix: Make the migration a staged value (Wrapped -> Committed -> StoreCleared) and on a delete failure do not roll back files for accounts whose OS copy is already deleted: either re-put the deleted keys into `inner` before removing wrapped files, or keep passcode enabled and retry the remaining deletes.

### 10-068: Manage Topics dialog Delete button deletes a topic and all its messages with no confirmation

`src/ui/groups_forum/mod.rs:636` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a forum's Manage Topics dialog (admin), click Delete on any non-General topic row.
- Effect: deleteForumTopic is sent at once and the topic with every message in it is gone; the context-menu path asks GroupConfirm::DeleteForumTopic first, this one does not. The note says 'Topic updated'.
- Fix: Route ForumTopicAction::Delete through open_group_confirm(DeleteForumTopic) like the topic menu; keep forum_topic_action for the non-destructive actions.

### 12-049: Quick reply `/` row sends to the main window's open chat, not the chat of the window it was picked in

`src/ui/quick_replies/mod.rs:401` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Main window has private chat A open; open private chat B in its own chat window; type `/` in B's composer (the menu is built from session.open_chat = A) and pick a shortcut
- Effect: The shortcut's messages are sent to chat A instead of B (or nothing when the main window has no chat open)
- Fix: Resolve the target with viewed_chat() (window scope) in send_quick_reply_row, command_menu_state and sync_command_menu, or carry the ChatId in the menu item.

### 13-140: Close-friends editor opened before the list is loaded starts from an empty base; Save then overwrites the server list with only the toggled users

`src/ui/story_more.rs:452` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: open Edit close friends right after sign-in or on a slow link, tick one contact before getClosefriends answers, press Save
- Effect: tick_close_friends adopts the loaded list only while !edit.changed(); the edit is already changed so it is never rebased, and setCloseFriends sends just the ticked ids, dropping all existing close friends
- Fix: CloseFriendsStage { Loading, Editing(edit) }: no checkboxes or Save until the list arrived

## S2: stuck state, cross-owner leak, wrong target, loop (61)

### 01-013: download_file copies a chat's file to the agent's path after access was revoked or turned off during the download

`src/agent/tools/people.rs:173` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: An agent calls download_file on a large file (waits up to 5 minutes). While it downloads the user turns Agent access off, revokes that chat's grant, or revokes the agent's connection. The download finishes.
- Effect: The file is copied to the agent's chosen folder although the user withdrew access. Only a lock is checked (locked_since_call_began); still_allowed (access generation, grants generation, revoked connection) is never called, unlike every write tool.
- Fix: Call still_allowed() after call_waiting returns and before copy_new, and treat download_file as a write-class call (or re-run require(Scope::Read) after the wait).

### 02-075: Login review counter stays above zero when a send fails mid-loop

`src/connect/account_hygiene.rs:66` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Two or more unconfirmed logins are listed; the user presses 'Yes, it's me' / 'No' and the sender fails on the 2nd or later confirmSession/terminateSession.
- Effect: review_pending is set to ids.len() but the error path calls finish_login_review once (decrements by 1) and returns; the ids never sent never answer, so review_pending stays > 0, the alert shows busy for good and review_unconfirmed_sessions is refused (needs restart).
- Fix: Track the review as one enum (Idle / Reviewing{outstanding: BTreeSet<RequestId>} / Failed / Done) keyed by request ids; a failed send removes every unsent id.

### 09-X013: review_pending sticks above zero when one of several confirm/terminate sends fails, disabling the new-login banner for good

`src/connect/account_hygiene.rs:66` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Two or more unconfirmed logins are listed in the 'Someone just got access' banner (account_notices.rs unconfirmed_login_banner); press 'Yes, it's me' or 'No, it's not me!' while the TDLib sender fails on the first or a middle send.
- Effect: review_pending is set to ids.len() up front; a failed send calls finish_login_review once and returns, so the requests never sent are never counted down. The banner's busy = review_pending > 0 stays true: both buttons stay disabled until restart, and review_outcome never comes.
- Fix: One enum `LoginReview { Idle, Running { confirmed, left: BTreeSet<RequestId>, error }, Done(LoginReviewOutcome) }`; on a failed send drop every id not yet sent from `left` before finishing.

### 02-082: Sign-in screen re-asks getCountries/getCountryCode every frame when the answer is empty or an error

`src/connect/auth.rs:117` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open the sign-in phone screen (WaitPhoneNumber) with no network, a TDLib error, an empty country list, or a getCountryCode text that is not two letters.
- Effect: frame_upkeep runs sync_signin every frame, which calls fetch_countries and fetch_country_code. The guards are 'countries.is_some()' / 'guessed_country_iso.is_some()' and have_purpose; the Countries arm only stores a non-empty list, the Text arm only a valid ISO, and GetCountries/GetCountryCode have no error arm, so after each answer the next frame sends the request again (request loop at round-trip rate).
- Fix: Hold both as Fetch<T> (Idle/Loading/Ready/Failed) so a finished-but-empty or failed answer is not 'ask again'.

### 02-083: Login-URL button context is one session slot, not matched to the request that asked

`src/connect/bots.rs:393` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Press login-URL button A in a bot message, then button B (same or another chat) before A's getLoginUrlInfo answers.
- Effect: send_login_url_info/send_login_url overwrite bots.login_url_request; the answer for A is paired with B's chat/message/button_id/raw_url, so the confirmation box shows A's domain but 'Allow' calls getLoginUrl for B's button (authorises the wrong link). Errors and last_login_url_info are single slots too; a failed send leaves a stale request in the slot.
- Fix: Put the LoginUrlRequest into the RequestPurpose (GetLoginUrlInfo{request}) so the answer carries its own context, and keep at most one box per button.

### 03-059: Starting another purchase (or opening another resale) overwrites a buy whose sendResoldGift is still out

`src/connect/gift_market.rs:168` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Confirm a resale purchase (sending=true), press Back (after_market_pop keeps a sending buy), tap a second listing in the still open Resale list, or open another gift's resale list (market.buy = None).
- Effect: The first purchase's answer is dropped (buy.request mismatch): Stars are spent but no 'Gift Sent' toast, the listing stays in the list, a craft purchase never fills its slot, and a failure is silent.
- Fix: Keep in-flight purchases as Vec<ResaleBuy> keyed by request id, or refuse start_gift_resale_buy/open_gift_resale while a buy is sending.

### 02-133: switch_language sets state.switching then returns early on a send error and nothing clears it

`src/connect/language.rs:87` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Pick a language while the TDLib sender fails once (closing/unavailable client): add_custom_server_language_pack, set option or getLanguagePackStrings send returns Err via ?
- Effect: settings.language.switching stays Some with no strings coming; language_ui.rs:204 treats it as 'in flight' so picking the same language again returns early; the reload path (drain_language) also waits for switching.is_none(). Stuck until restart/account switch
- Fix: Set switching only after all requests are sent, or clear it (and drop the generation) in the Err arm; model as enum Switch { Idle, Switching{generation,..} }

### 03-014: Video-note story reply runs ffmpeg synchronously on the UI thread

`src/connect/stories/reply_media.rs:139` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Record a round video as a story reply and press Send (ui/story_viewer/reply_media.rs:457 calls this inside this.update_in).
- Effect: write_video_note_thumbnail waits for ffmpeg (up to PROBE_LIMIT*3 = 30 s) on the main thread; the whole window freezes during the wait, and with a hung ffmpeg for half a minute.
- Fix: Make the thumbnail with the capture finish on the background task and pass it in the draft; the driver only sends.

### 03-040: A timed-out export request leaves WorkerLink::pending set, so its late answer is handed to the worker's next call

`src/export/handle.rs:56` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Run a full account export with several chats; one chat's getChatHistory/searchChatMessages/download takes over 300 s (QUERY_TIMEOUT) to answer. ChannelTdlib::call returns Tdlib("the request timed out"), export_chats counts that chat as failed and carries on (account/worker.rs:482). The driver still has pending=Some(A) and forwards nothing else; when A's answer arrives WorkerLink::reply sends it down the id-less replies channel, where the next chat's first call takes it as its own answer.
- Effect: The next chat is exported with the previous chat's messages (or every later answer is shifted by one), so a chat folder holds another chat's content under the wrong name; later calls misparse until the export ends.
- Fix: Make the reply channel carry the RequestId and have ChannelTdlib::call discard replies that are not its own request; on timeout also clear/abandon the link's pending entry (an enum ExportCall {Idle, Awaiting(RequestId), Abandoned(RequestId)}).

### 05-017: Star pack answer for one gift recipient is applied under another recipient (answer not matched to what asked)

`src/stars_gift.rs:18` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Gift Stars: pick contact A (getStarGiftPaymentOptions in flight), press Back, pick contact B (second request); A's answer lands after B's, or Back is pressed and A's answer lands on the contact list
- Effect: B's checkout page lists the packs fetched for A (apply.rs:274 sets options and loading=false for any GetStarGiftOptions answer; RequestPurpose::GetStarGiftOptions carries no user id); loading=false is cleared by the first answer while B's is still outstanding. Stars are bought for B with A's pack amounts
- Fix: Carry the target user id in the purpose (GetStarGiftOptions { user_id }) and apply the answer only when stage is Packs for that user; the enum from the IS entry holds the key.

### 03-058: A finished gift transfer reports the contact of the picker's current stage, not of the request

`src/state/domains/payments/gift_manage.rs:121` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Transfer a collectible to Ann (confirm). While transferGift is out, press Cancel (allowed: back() only refuses during export), pick Bob so the picker shows Confirm for Bob; the answer for Ann arrives.
- Effect: The toast says the gift went to Bob and Quill opens Bob's chat; if the picker was left on the list or closed, no toast and no chat at all. The gift went to Ann.
- Fix: Keep the recipient in the in-flight request (PaymentsPurpose::TransferGift { user_id, gift_name }) and build GiftTransferred from it, or make the picker stage Transferring { user_id } that back() refuses.

### 06-001: A failed older page of a comment thread is re-requested at round-trip speed

`src/state/session_thread.rs:226` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a channel post's comments (or a group reply thread), scroll to the top of the loaded replies so the older page is requested, and have getMessageThreadHistory fail (network error, swept as lost after its TTL, or a persistent TDLib error such as losing access to the discussion group).
- Effect: fail_thread only records a failure for the newer side (newer_failed) or for a thread that is not Ready yet. An older-page failure of a Ready thread leaves no mark, loaded_complete stays false, and fetch_thread_history (called from maybe_auto_load_older on every render with row 0 shown, and from preload_history_by_scroll) sends the same page again as soon as the answer lands: a request loop with no Retry row, unlike the main history's load_failed.
- Fix: Give each end of the thread window a page state (Idle \| Loading{request} \| Failed) on TopicHistory, set Failed in fail_thread for the older end and have fetch_thread_history wait for an explicit Retry, as the main history's load_failed does.

### 06-002: A failed older page of a forum topic is re-requested in a loop

`src/state/topic_window.rs:239` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a forum topic with messages loaded, scroll to the top so GetTopicHistory asks for an older page, and have it fail (network error, a 408 from the sweep, or a persistent error).
- Effect: fail_topic_page marks topic_load_failed, but topic_history_load_failed only counts it while the topic has no messages, so fetch_topic_history's guard ignores it and maybe_auto_load_older (called each render while row 0 is shown) sends the page again as soon as the error lands. The pane shows no error and the request repeats while the user stays at the top.
- Fix: Track the older end's fetch as one value per topic window (Idle \| Loading \| Failed) and stop auto-paging in Failed until a Retry, instead of a failure set that only the first page reads.

### 06-050: updateChatPendingJoinRequests with a null pending_join_requests is a parse error, so the join-requests bar never clears

`src/telegram/envelope/domains/groups/parse.rs:708` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Be an admin of a group or channel with 'Approve new members' on and one pending join request, so the bar shows '1 request to join'. Approve or dismiss that request in Quill (one request, not 'all'), or let another admin or your phone handle it, or let the requester cancel. TDLib sends updateChatPendingJoinRequests with pending_join_requests: null (schema td_api.tl:11466 'may be null').
- Effect: The parser calls int53(None)? on total_count, returns MissingField, and copy_and_parse drops the update (an update has no @extra to recover). groups.pending_join_request_counts keeps the old count, so the bar stays with the old count and avatars. Opening it shows an empty list. Only 'approve all' (ProcessAllChatJoinRequests, session_apply_ok.rs:719) or a new request clears it; a single approve (forget_join_request) never touches the count. The bar stays until restart.
- Fix: Parse a null or absent pending_join_requests as total_count 0 with no user ids (UpdateChatPendingJoinRequests { total_count: 0, user_ids: vec![] }), and treat only a missing chat_id as a parse error.

### 07-001: Paid-chat guard cannot price media, album, voice/video note, checklist or plain forward sends: their builders write options:null and set_paid only fills an existing options object

`src/telegram/requests/media.rs:192` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a private chat with a user who charges Stars per message (or a paid channel DM). Attach a photo (or record a voice note, send a checklist, or forward messages from the share box without Silent/Schedule) and press Send. Confirm the 'Pay N Stars' box (or have 'Don't ask again' trust stored).
- Effect: PaidGuard::screen holds the request; release_paid_send sends HeldSend::with_price(), but set_paid (src/paid_messages_guard.rs:81-85) skips because options is null, so the send goes out with no paid_message_star_count. TDLib refuses it (payment required), and the message the user agreed to pay for fails. The trusted path (screen, :140-142) has the same no-op. Only text/dice/sticker/game sends, which build a messageSendOptions object, work. Tests (connect/tests/paid_messages_guard.rs) use hand-written JSON with an options object, so they miss it.
- Fix: Make set_paid write a full messageSendOptions object when options is null (or have every send builder always emit message_send_options), and test the guard with JSON from the real builders.

### 07-005: A text reply to a story sends clear_draft: true, so TDLib deletes the draft the user has in the poster's chat

`src/telegram/requests/stories.rs:262` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: 1. Open the private chat with Alice, type a message and leave it as a draft. 2. Open Alice's story from the story tray. 3. Type a reply in the story viewer's reply field and send it (ConnectDriver story reply, connect/stories/posting.rs:115 -> send_text_story_reply).
- Effect: The reply is sent to the chat with Alice, and TDLib also deletes that chat's draft (schema td_api.tl:6726 'Pass true to delete message draft in the chat'). The text typed in step 1 is gone. The story reply field is not that chat's composer; Quill already keeps the draft for sends that are not the composer's content (SendOptions::literal_text, composer/send.rs:104-107).
- Fix: Send clear_draft: false from send_text_story_reply (as the sticker/GIF/voice story replies and the dice already do), so only a send of the chat's own composer content clears its draft.

### 07-012: Hidden translate bars are app-wide and keyed by bare chat id, so hiding one leaks to other accounts

`src/translate/mod.rs:633` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: On account A, hide the translate bar in a channel or private chat (set_translate_bar_hidden -> TranslatePrefs::set_bar_hidden(chat_id)). Switch to account B and open the same peer, for example the same public channel, or B's private chat with A's user (which has the id of A's Saved Messages).
- Effect: B's translate bar for that chat is hidden too, although B never hid it. TranslatePrefs is the app's file (ui/translate_ui/mod.rs:118, settings/app_prefs.rs), while tdesktop keeps the hidden flag per account on the server.
- Fix: Key the hidden chats by owner (`Vec<AccountChat>` / `HashMap<AccountKey, Vec<ChatId>>`), or move them into the account's own prefs file.

### 07-035: Appearance page re-sends getInstalledBackgrounds on every render after it fails (request loop, 'Loading wallpapers' forever)

`src/ui/appearance/telegram_wallpapers.rs:209` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open Settings > Appearance while getInstalledBackgrounds answers with an error (e.g. a FLOOD_WAIT or any TDLib error) and keep the page open.
- Effect: The error arm (state/domains/chats/error.rs:37-46) only sets chats_state.background_error; installed_backgrounds stays None. ensure_wallpapers_loaded is called from the page's render (settings_window/pages.rs:98) and re-sends whenever installed_backgrounds is None and none is pending, so each error answer re-renders the page and sends the request again, while the gallery shows 'Loading wallpapers...' next to the 'Couldn't update wallpapers' line indefinitely.
- Fix: Hold the gallery as Fetch<Vec<Background>> (idle/loading/ready/failed) and request only from Idle (and on an explicit retry), not from render; background_error for the other eight purposes stays separate.

### 08-049: Report spam with unarchive also leaves the chat

`src/ui/chat_bars.rs:421` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Archived group shows a ReportSpam bar with can_unarchive: the button reads 'Report spam' (no leave). Press it and confirm OK.
- Effect: submit_bar_report always calls leave_channel after report_chat, so the user silently leaves the group/channel; for a private chat leaveChat is wrong or errors and the note says the report failed although it went out.
- Fix: Carry the bar variant into the confirm: an enum ReportSpamAction { ReportOnly, ReportAndLeave } chosen when the button is built and passed to submit_bar_report.

### 08-127: begin_edit while already editing overwrites the stashed draft and reply

`src/ui/composer/copy_inline_text.rs:160` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Type a draft (with a reply bar) in chat A, press Up or choose Edit on message M1, then right-click message M2 and choose Edit (no is_editing guard in message_menu_overlay.rs:463 or scheduled.rs:805).
- Effect: begin_edit reads the field (M1's edit text) as 'current', calls note_open_draft(false) and enter_edit replaces edit_mode whole: the real draft and stashed reply are gone, M1's edited text becomes the stashed draft. Esc/finish restores M1's text as the chat draft; the original draft is lost.
- Fix: begin_edit on an active edit first leaves the old edit (clear_edit) or carries the old EditMode's stash into the new one; make enter_edit take the previous EditMode and keep its stash.

### 09-X047: Instant View is session-wide state drawn in every window

`src/ui/esc_stack.rs:47` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a chat in its own window, click an Instant View link there.
- Effect: session.messages.instant_view is drawn by both app_render/mod.rs:580 and chat_window/body.rs:149, so the page covers the main window and every chat window; Escape in any window (it is in WINDOW_LAYERS) closes it in all; instant_view_fallback_url is taken and opened by whichever window renders first.
- Fix: Record the asking window with the page (InstantViewOpen{window, page}) or move the open page into the WindowSlot, and draw/close it only there.

### 09-104: Save in the edit-folder box while the folder is still loading sends editChatFolder with an empty editor

`src/ui/folders/save_folder_editor.rs:40` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Settings > Folders > click a folder whose cached spec is stale (or while offline / slow), type a name in the name field shown under 'Loading folder', click Save before getChatFolder answers.
- Effect: save_folder_editor validates only the name, then edit_chat_folder(id, FolderEditor::new().to_spec()) replaces the folder with no included chats, no pinned chats and no type rules: the folder's contents are wiped on the server. Save is never disabled by fetch_pending.
- Fix: Make the editor an enum Loading/Failed/Ready(FolderEditor) (or Fetch<ChatFolderSpec>); Save exists only in Ready.

### 09-X061: Auction info box re-sends getGiftUpgradePreview on every render after it fails

`src/ui/gift_auction_ui.rs:210` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a gift auction's info box (link or tile) for a gift whose upgrade preview request fails (error from TDLib, or offline).
- Effect: auction_info_box calls fetch_gift_upgrade_preview each render; the error arm for UpgradePreview stores nothing (gift_market.rs apply error is a no-op), so `previews` never gets the key and the request goes out again every redraw, at least once a second from the box's 1 fps tick, for as long as the box is open.
- Fix: Keep the preview as `Fetch<GiftPreview>` per gift id (failed is a stage), ask from an open action not from render, and do not retry a failed fetch until the box reopens.

### 09-122: Raising a bid placed for a channel from Active Auctions re-places it for the user

`src/ui/gift_auction_ui.rs:564` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Have a bid whose owner is a channel (MessageSender::Chat); open the chat-list auction bar or Active Auctions and tap Raise, then Place Bid.
- Effect: raise_active_bid sets gift_market.recipient = None for a Chat owner, auction_recipient() falls back to the user, bid_gift_auction sees a different owner and sends placeGiftAuctionBid for the user: the Stars go to a bid for the wrong recipient and the channel bid is replaced.
- Fix: Carry the bid's owner as the recipient (an enum of User/Chat) and refuse or route a Chat owner to ChangeRecipient instead of dropping to None.

### 03-060: Esc or close during the forge drops the craft session and the craft's outcome

`src/ui/gift_craft_ui.rs:311` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Press Craft (craftGift sent, gifts burn), then Escape or the dialog close button while the forge animation runs.
- Effect: market_back pops the Craft box, after_craft_pop sets market.craft = None; the CraftResult answer is filtered out (craft_request mismatch), so the user never learns whether the craft succeeded or the gifts were lost.
- Fix: Refuse the pop while craft.crafting (CraftStage::Crafting), or keep the session until its answer and show the result as a toast when the box is gone.

### 10-001: Form stays open after a new giveaway is paid, so Start can be paid a second time

`src/ui/giveaway_create_ui.rs:650` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a channel's boosts page > Get Boosts via Gifts > pick a Premium/Stars option > Start Giveaway > pay in the checkout > close the paid screen.
- Effect: The Giveaway dialog (is_open = form.is_some() && !payment_dialog_visible) comes back with the same draft and an enabled Start Giveaway; nothing sets launch=Launched or takes the form after a payment (pay_new_giveaway only sets a note). The user can open the checkout again and buy a second giveaway.
- Fix: Make the form an enum (Editing, AwaitingPayment{invoice}, Paid, Launching, Launched) and move it to Paid/close it when the checkout's paid answer for that invoice arrives.

### 10-078: Promote picker keeps a selection that the search results no longer show

`src/ui/group_moderation/promote_dialog_panel.rs:131` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open Promote admin, tick member A, type another name and press Search so A is not in the new page, then press Promote.
- Effect: No radio is selected (selected_ix is None) but selected_user is still A and the Promote button stays enabled, so A is promoted to administrator with the rights shown, unseen.
- Fix: Clear selected_user in search_promote_members, and enable Promote only when selected_user is among the candidates shown (derive from the page, not stored).

### 10-119: begin_inline_return is set before the bot chat resolves and is never cancelled when that fails

`src/ui/inline_grid.rs:455` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Tap the inline results button 'Start' while the lookup fails (resolve_deep_link Err, or the username later fails to resolve), then later open the bot's chat by any route.
- Effect: take_inline_start finds the stale pending_start and sends '/start <parameter>' to the bot unprompted; the origin chat may also be navigated back to when a switch-inline message arrives hours later.
- Fix: InlineReturn becomes an enum Opening{..} -> Started{baseline}; clear it on resolve failure, chat change, account switch or a timeout of the open request.

### 11-p3-001: Music row play button uses the main window's open chat, not the message's chat

`src/ui/message_media/audio.rs:429` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a chat with a music file in a chat window (pop-out) while the main window shows another chat or none; press play on the music row.
- Effect: With no main chat open the button does nothing. With another chat open, playback starts under that other chat's id (player.chat, positions key), so the row never shows as playing and a same-numbered message of the other chat is toggled.
- Fix: Pass the message's ChatId into audio_row (as voice_note_row does) and use it; never read open_chat_id inside a row that can draw in a chat window.

### 11-p7-001: Checkout pays with a stale validated order after the fields are edited

`src/ui/payments/mod.rs:246` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open an invoice that needs a shipping address, fill it, press Continue (shipping options appear), then edit the street or country and press Pay without pressing Continue again.
- Effect: Pay is enabled (order_ok only checks validated.is_some()) and submit_payment sends the old order_info_id and shipping option, so the order is charged and shipped against the address and price that were validated, not the ones shown.
- Fix: Make order state one enum on the dialog: Unvalidated, Validating, Validated{order_info_id, shipping options, snapshot of the OrderInfoData}; Pay requires Validated whose snapshot equals the current fields, and any field edit drops back to Unvalidated.

### 12-001: Edited-photo render never lands after an account switch: tray card stays 'rendering' and a held Send waits forever

`src/ui/photo_editor_save.rs:121` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: In account A attach a photo, open the editor, press Done (render starts, tray card gets photo_render=Some(n)); before the PNG lands switch to account B. The task's screen epoch is stale, so update_in fails and the fallback app.main().update (which keeps the same stale screen epoch) also fails; the PNG is deleted. Press Send in A first so a Send is held, or switch back to A later.
- Effect: Back in A the card is stuck rendering (photo_render never cleared anywhere else), tray_renders_an_edit() is true so every Send is parked as held_send with 'Preparing the files' and never goes, and Edit on the card defers forever (photo_editor_after). Only removing the card frees it.
- Fix: Make the card's state an enum (Ready / Rendering{token}) owned by the tray, and settle it from the dropped continuation: on Gone fall back to ScopedApp::app() (no screen) to land_attachment_edit(None) instead of main(), which keeps the stale epoch.

### 13-064: Appearance page asks for installed backgrounds on every render, so a failed answer starts a request loop

`src/ui/settings_window/pages.rs:98` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open Settings > Appearance while TDLib answers getInstalledBackgrounds with an error (flood wait, server error). The error sets chats_state.background_error but leaves installed_backgrounds None; the answer re-renders the page, ensure_wallpapers_loaded sees None and no pending request, and sends the request again.
- Effect: A request plus redraw loop at network round-trip speed for as long as the page is open; the wallpaper section never leaves its loading look.
- Fix: Hold the gallery as Fetch<Vec<Background>> (idle, loading, ready, failed) and ask only from idle on page arrival (switch_settings_page), not from render; Failed offers a retry.

### 13-044: Similar-channels strip re-requests getChatSimilarChats every frame after a failed fetch

`src/ui/similar_strip.rs:179` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a joined channel whose getChatSimilarChats fails (flood wait, API error). Render calls similar_channels_strip, similar_strip_tiles returns None for ProfileChatsFetch::Failed, request_similar_chats defers fetch_similar_chats, and fetch_profile_chats skips only Loading and Loaded, so Failed is sent again.
- Effect: Each failure notifies, redraws and sends the request again: a request loop paced by the round trip, which deepens a flood wait. The strip never shows.
- Fix: Treat Failed as terminal in the draw path: ask only when the fetch is absent, and retry only from an explicit action or after a changed key.

### 13-022: Statistics panel spins forever when the fetch is skipped because can_get_statistics is not known yet

`src/ui/statistics.rs:128` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open the Statistics panel on a channel before supergroupFullInfo has loaded (or via the message menu 'Statistics' on a fresh chat). fetch_chat_statistics returns Ok(None) without inserting any entry; nothing refetches when the full info arrives.
- Effect: 'Loading statistics, it can take a few minutes' forever; also for a chat whose full info says can_get_statistics=false.
- Fix: Idle/Unavailable variants in ChatStatisticsFetch; draw None as 'not available' or retrigger the fetch when the full info arrives.

### 13-021: Refresh (or Retry) removes the cached statistics before the send can fail, leaving a spinner with no retry

`src/ui/statistics.rs:349` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open channel statistics, lose the connection (chats_path inactive) or have the request refused, press Refresh. refresh_chat_statistics removes the entry first, then fetch_chat_statistics returns Err.
- Effect: Panel shows 'Loading statistics' spinner forever (None is drawn like Loading, no Retry button) until the panel is closed and reopened; the loaded numbers are gone.
- Fix: Do not remove the entry; set it to Loading only after a successful send, or to Failed on Err. Draw None as Idle with a Load button.

### 13-144: Viewers list re-requests a failed page every frame

`src/ui/story_viewer/icon_chrome.rs:583` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: open Viewers on a story with more than one page, scroll to the end so page 2 fails (network drop, flood wait)
- Effect: fail_story_viewers sets error and loading=false but keeps next_offset, so story_viewers_scrolled (run each frame from story_frame_upkeep and on scroll) sees more==true and sends the same request again, and again after each failure: a request loop at round-trip rate
- Fix: Fetch-like enum for the page (Idle\|Loading\|Failed) so a Failed page waits for scroll or Retry

### 13-089: sync_live_story marks the live story as watched before the join, and a failed join is never rolled back or retried

`src/ui/story_viewer/live_story.rs:54` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a live story while the join cannot start (no call engine, the story not cached as Live yet, or any join_live_story Err such as InvalidRequest/CallsUnavailable). sync_live_story sets stories.live.watching = target, then join_watched_live_story only sets a note on Err.
- Effect: watching == the shown story, so live_story_media draws the empty live frame (spinner state, no player) and the 'Join live' button in story_viewer_overlay never shows; sync does not rejoin because watching.is_some(); the Retry overlay only appears for a tracked failed call (join_failed), which never exists. The user sees a stuck opening spinner until they step away or close the viewer.
- Fix: Make the live join one enum on LiveStoryUi (Idle, Joining{story}, Joined{story}, Failed{story,err}); set Joining only when join_live_story returns Ok, set Failed on Err so the overlay shows Retry/Join, and let sync retry from Idle.

### 13-104: advance_thread notifies every frame while a thread page request is in flight

`src/ui/threads.rs:202` · bug · confirmed

- Trigger: Open a comment thread, click the root bar (ScrollGoal::ThreadRoot) while older replies are unloaded, or have reload_needed set after an own send; go slow/offline so the getMessageThreadHistory answer is late.
- Effect: fetch_thread_history returns Ok(None) when the request is already pending (dedupe) and does not clear reload_needed / scroll goal, so is_ok() is true and cx.notify() runs each render: a redraw loop (render calls advance_thread, notify schedules another render) for as long as the page is in flight, and forever if the answer never comes.
- Fix: Make fetch_thread_history return an enum (Sent, AlreadyPending, Done) and notify only on Sent; or let the answer, not the poll, drive the redraw.

### 01-085: KeyFilter keeps its own held flag; a missed key-up leaves it stuck and the microphone open

`src/calls/ptt_global.rs:104` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Join a group call with push-to-talk and the system-wide hook on; hold the key; the hook misses the release (macOS event tap disabled by timeout and re-enabled in on_event, Windows on_key try_lock fails because CONTEXT is locked by a starting/stopping hook thread, X11 record gap); release the key. Because the hook is active, group_call_ptt_release_all returns early and never resets.
- Effect: KeyFilter.held stays true and PushToTalk.held/talking stay true: the microphone stays open (transmitting to the call) with no key down. The next press is swallowed (held == pressed returns None), the next release finally sends Up.
- Fix: Resync on hook gaps: drop the filter and send a synthetic Up when the tap is re-enabled or an event is skipped, or poll the real key state; one KeyState enum owned by the hook with a reset edge.

### 02-062: open_send_gift_to replaces the whole send state while a sendGift is in flight

`src/connect/gift_manage.rs:524` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Press Send in the send-gift dialog, close the dialog (close_send_gift keeps sending=true), reopen the dialog for any user before the answer.
- Effect: SendGiftState is reset (sending=false, request=None), so the Send button is live again and the first answer is dropped by waits_for; a second Send spends Stars again and the first outcome is never shown.
- Fix: Refuse to open (or keep the in-flight purchase) while send is Sending{request}; model send as enum Idle/Sending/Done/Failed.

### 01-051: open_boost_box replaces the box but leaves boost_intent and stashed slots of the old chat

`src/connect/groups/boost_flow.rs:31` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: In the boost box of channel A press Boost (getAvailableChatBoostSlots out, boost_intent=A), then open a t.me/B?boost link so open_boost_box(B) swaps the box before the slots answer arrives.
- Effect: The slots answer is stashed for A (intent still A) and maybe_continue_boost sends boostChat for A with a free slot while the user is looking at B's box: a boost is spent on a chat the screen no longer shows.
- Fix: Fold intent into the box state (BoostPhase::Working carries the chat and request id) or clear boost_intent and boost_slots_by_chat whenever boost_box is replaced, as close_boost_box does.

### 02-158: Undo forgets the waiting Stars locally even if removePendingPaidMessageReactions was not sent

`src/connect/paid_reaction.rs:53` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Tap a Star reaction, press Undo while the sender refuses (send_json_request Err).
- Effect: paid.undo() already dropped the entry, TDLib still holds the pending Stars and may commit them later; the toast says undone while Stars can still be spent.
- Fix: Remove the local entry only after the send succeeds, or keep an Undoing stage until TDLib answers.

### 02-152: submit_payment_form has no in-flight guard, so a double Pay can send sendPaymentForm twice

`src/connect/payments.rs:68` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Open a Stars checkout, click Pay twice quickly (two click events before the next render). submit_stars_payment (src/ui/invoice_checkout.rs:193) checks form_paid but not checkout_sending; the driver does not check is_paying(invoice) either.
- Effect: Two sendPaymentForm requests for one invoice: Stars may be spent twice.
- Fix: Refuse in the driver when is_paying(invoice) (one Payment stage enum per checkout: Idle, Forming, Validating, Paying, Paid).

### 04-057: Local pending Star reactions are dropped before TDLib confirms commit/undo, so TDLib's pending total can exceed what the user saw

`src/paid_reaction.rs:278` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Tap the Star reaction (addPending sent, local entry kept). When the 5.5 s timer ends, take_due removes the entry and the commit request fails (send error or error answer), or tap Undo and removePendingPaidMessageReactions fails. Tap the same message again.
- Effect: Only a note is set ('Stars were not sent' / 'Couldn't undo the Stars'). TDLib still holds the earlier pending Stars for that message, local state says none, so the next tap shows a toast and balance check for the new amount only while the commit that follows spends old + new Stars. The user pays more Stars than the toast showed. The refusal/balance check (total_pending) also ignores the lost reservation.
- Fix: Make each pending item an enum PendingPaid { Waiting, Committing(request), Undoing(request) } that leaves the list only on TDLib's ok; on error return it to Waiting (or issue removePending) so local and TDLib totals agree.

### 04-080: An existing but unreadable accounts.json loads as the default registry, and the next save overwrites the real file

`src/settings/accounts.rs:146` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: accounts.json exists but fs::read fails once (AV lock on Windows, EIO, permission blip); then add account / switch account / toggle notify-from-all calls load_registry then save_registry
- Effect: load_registry hits `None => return AccountRegistry::default()` (stamp is Some), the caller saves [primary] plus its change over the real list: every other account and any pending_deletes vanish from the registry, their folders are orphaned and need a fresh sign-in
- Fix: Make load return Result<Loaded, Unreadable>/an enum {Missing, Corrupt, Unreadable, Ok}; read-modify-write callers refuse to save on Unreadable instead of starting from the default.

### 05-002: finished is set by the caller after the worker returns, so a panicking worker leaves the manager polling forever

`src/spell_download.rs:41` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Download a dictionary whose files make spellbook or ureq panic in the background task (malformed upstream data).
- Effect: Row stays at Downloading n% and the 120 ms poll loop notifies every tick; start_dictionary_download returns early because download is Some, so no dictionary can be downloaded until restart.
- Fix: Set finished from a drop guard in the worker, or drive the row from the worker's Result (JoinHandle) rather than a flag; clear download on any task end.

### 08-071: Pinned drag with a lost mouse-up never ends and redraws at 60 Hz

`src/ui/chat_list/sidebar.rs:325` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Start dragging a pinned chat and release the mouse outside the window, or lose the release to a menu/alert/window deactivation, so neither on_mouse_up nor on_mouse_up_out on #chat-rows fires.
- Effect: PinReorder::animating() stays true while dragging is Some, so sidebar_chats never clears it and calls request_animation_tick(60) every frame; the list stays in the swapped order and the grabbing cursor stays. The `!window_active && dragging().is_none()` escape does not cover the dragging case.
- Fix: Cancel the reorder on drag end/cancel (GPUI drag-ended hook), window deactivation and Esc; treat Reordering as an enum stage with those exits.

### 09-X012: Closing a chat window that hosts the account notice box dismisses up to 8 queued notices unseen, including a force-logout one

`src/ui/dialogs/account_notices.rs:549` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Click anything in a chat window; two or more service notices arrive (updateServiceNotification), the first a force-logout (AUTH_KEY_DROP_*) notice; the AccountNotice box shows in that chat window; close the chat window with its title-bar close or Cmd-W.
- Effect: close_window_boxes runs the box's close (dismiss_account_notice) while account_notice() is Some, up to 8 times: every queued service notice, the login-prevented box and the frozen/age boxes are popped without being shown, and the force-logout notice (whose own box cannot be dismissed) goes away without the log out it demands.
- Fix: Give AccountNotice a close that closes the box view, not the notices (a front-notice cursor that is only advanced by its own buttons), or register it without a close so it is freed to the window of the last press like the call swap prompt; never dismiss a force-logout notice from a close.

### 09-050: Bulk 'Delete chats' confirmation deletes the live selection at submit time, not the chats shown when asked

`src/ui/dialogs/group_confirm.rs:48` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Select several chats, press Delete (confirm shows 'N chats'), change the selection (Enter on the focused list toggles a row via chat_list_keys.rs:461, or an agent/other path) before pressing Delete in the box.
- Effect: The ids are read from chat_list.selected at confirm (groups/open_custom_title_dialog.rs:288-301), so chats the user never saw in the question are removed from the list and their history deleted.
- Fix: Carry the ids in the action (RemoveSelectedChats{ids: Vec<ChatId>} snapshotted at ask) so the answer is matched to what asked.

### 09-054: Dropping a folder on the FolderFiles zone walks the directory tree on the UI thread

`src/ui/drop_zones.rs:97` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Drag a folder that lives on a slow or disconnected network share over the conversation and drop it on the 'files in folder' zone.
- Effect: drop_on_zone calls folder_files_for_sending (read_dir of the folder and of every subfolder, plus file_type per entry) synchronously in the click handler; the window freezes until the share answers. The drag probe a few lines up was moved off the UI thread for the same reason.
- Fix: Run folder_files_for_sending through run_off_ui_scoped_then and apply the plan when it lands, keyed by the slot.

### 09-110: drive_folder_invite re-sends getChat for unresolved link chats on every poll

`src/ui/folder_share/mod.rs:849` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Open an addlist link whose missing/added chats TDLib cannot return from getChat (inaccessible chat, error answer).
- Effect: fetch_chats_for_folder_invite is called from every poll tick while info is set; its only dedup is has_purpose_for_chat for requests still pending, and GetFolderInviteChat has no error arm or tombstone, so a failed getChat is re-sent at poll rate for as long as the box is open.
- Fix: Record asked chat ids in the dialog (HashSet or per-chat Fetch) and ask once.

### 09-X052: Save is live while the editor is still loading, and sends a blank spec for an existing folder

`src/ui/folders/save_folder_editor.rs:9` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Open Edit for a folder whose spec must be fetched; before it arrives type a name into the visible name field and click Save.
- Effect: save_folder_editor ignores fetch_pending and sends editChatFolder with FolderEditor::new() (no chats, no types, default icon) under the typed name; the box closes with 'Folder updated'. TDLib most likely refuses an empty folder, so the user sees success while nothing (or a wiped folder) was saved.
- Fix: With the editor content as Fetch<FolderEditor>, Save exists only in the Ready variant (disabled otherwise).

### 09-123: Closing the gift market box during the forge drops the craft and discards the craftGift result

`src/ui/gift_craft_ui.rs:299` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Craft with 2+ gifts; while the forge animation runs press Escape or the dialog close (forge_box has no footer but the stack is still popped by market_back).
- Effect: after_market_pop -> after_craft_pop sets session craft to None while crafting; apply of CraftResult filters on craft_request so the answer is dropped. The gifts are burnt by Telegram but the user never sees the new gift or the failure.
- Fix: Make the forge a stage of one enum (Idle/Forging{request}/Done(result)) that the box cannot pop while Forging, or keep the result and toast it when the box is gone.

### 10-087: Bulk 'delete chats' acts on the selection at submit time, not the one the user confirmed

`src/ui/groups/open_custom_title_dialog.rs:288` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Select chats, press Delete, and while the confirm box is open the list changes (a selected chat is archived or removed, another listed chat arrives and is re-listed in the selection).
- Effect: selected_chat_ids() is recomputed in submit_group_confirm; the confirm text names no chats and the enum is Copy, so the set deleted is whatever is selected then. Also the note reports success when 0 requests were sent.
- Fix: Carry the chat ids in the confirm variant (Vec) and show the count; report an error when none was sent.

### 10-121: Failed next-page inline query is re-asked on every poll batch

`src/ui/inline_grid.rs:525` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Scroll an inline results panel to its end; the next_offset request fails (first_page=false error leaves the slot Loaded).
- Effect: should_load_more is still true and nothing is in flight, so progress_inline_mode_here (every poll_live batch, including the error's) sends it again: a request loop while the panel is open.
- Fix: Record a failed offset on the slot (as inline_members does with failed_offset) and retry only on the next scroll.

### 10-152: submit_stars_payment has no in-flight guard; only the render disables the button

`src/ui/invoice_checkout.rs:193` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Double-click Pay on a Stars/gift/giveaway form faster than a frame
- Effect: Two sendPaymentForm for a non-message invoice (payment_extra does not dedupe); Stars may be charged twice if the server accepts both
- Fix: Check checkout_sending()/Paying stage in submit_stars_payment itself

### 11-p6-003: Ownership transfer answer is not matched to the dialog that asked

`src/ui/ownership.rs:197` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Transfer ownership of chat A (Change owner, answer slow). Close the box, then open Leave on chat B and press Appoint another owner then pick a user, so B's dialog is Confirm{leave_after:true}. A's transferred answer lands.
- Effect: leave_after is read from whatever dialog is open now, not from the transfer's chat. Quill leaves chat A the user meant to keep, drops B's dialog, and the note names A.
- Fix: Carry the chat id and the leave_after choice in the in-flight transfer (one Transfer{chat, user, leave_after} value) and apply the answer only to it; a dialog for another chat is untouched.

### 02-160: The Star reaction box (chat_id, message_id) lives on QuillApp and is not reset on an account switch

`src/ui/paid_reaction_ui.rs:170` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Open the Send Stars box in account A, switch to account B (shortcut or menu) and press Send.
- Effect: send_paid_box uses A's chat and message ids with B's driver: Stars from B may go to the wrong or missing message. Only closers in paid_reaction_ui.rs set boxed = None; no Farewell closer was found.
- Fix: Move the box into OnScreen (per account) so the swap replaces it, with an OfAccount owner key.

### 12-086: Delete-selection and single-delete alert boxes are not tied to the account/chat that opened them

`src/ui/selection_mode.rs:596` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Select messages in account A, click Delete N so the alert opens, then switch account (shortcut, notification click, deep link) and press Delete
- Effect: The on_ok closure applies the captured chat_id/ids to whatever live driver is on screen; a private chat id shared by two accounts deletes message ids in the wrong account (the kit alert is not a registered DialogKind, so no Farewell closes it)
- Fix: Register the alert as a DialogKind owned by OnScreen (or carry an AccountKey and compare on ok) so a switch closes it.

### 13-045: Sign-in phone screen resends getCountries and getCountryCode every frame when the answer is empty, invalid or an error

`src/ui/signin_ui.rs:168` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: Open the sign-in phone step behind a VPN or an IP TDLib cannot place. getCountryCode answers an empty string, or either request errors. apply drops an empty country list and a non 2-letter code, the pending request is gone, and sync_signin (every frame via frame_upkeep) calls fetch_countries and fetch_country_code again.
- Effect: A request, answer, redraw, request loop while the phone screen is up.
- Fix: Make each lookup a Fetch<T> in AuthState (idle, loading, ready, failed) so a settled answer, empty or failed, is not asked again until the screen is reopened.

### 13-057: recheck_spelling asks the spell backend on the UI thread

`src/ui/spellcheck_ui.rs:318` · bug · plausible

- Trigger: On macOS with the NSSpellChecker worker stuck, use Add to Dictionary, Remove from Dictionary or Ignore. recheck_spelling calls checker.is_correct for every underlined word of every window after the cache was cleared; the macOS backend waits CHECK_WAIT (5 s) per uncached word on the main thread. build_composer_menu also calls is_correct and suggestions there.
- Effect: The window freezes for seconds per word.
- Fix: Drop the word from the list by its text and let schedule_spellcheck re-check in the background.

| 11-p4-009 | `src/ui/message_poll.rs:451` | bug | p | Rapid taps on a multiple-answer poll lose the first choice | Keep a per-poll vote stage (Idle \| Sending{chosen, request}) in the session and build the next set from the pending chosen set; ignore taps while a quiz answer is in flight. |
| 11-p4-002 | `src/ui/message_state.rs:35` | bug | p | A held history press that never sees its mouse-up keeps a 15 ms poll loop and a stale drag alive | Own the drag in a ChatUi value that sync_chat_ui replaces, clear it in close_for_lock, and end it on a MouseMove with no button pressed. |
| 11-p5-008 | `src/ui/notification_settings/desktop_options.rs:239` | bug | p | Corner preview windows end only on hover-out | Own the preview as one value (Option<ScreenCorner>) cleared when the page closes, or give the samples a drop/close hook. |

| 11-p5-015 | `src/ui/notification_settings/mute_time.rs:60` | bug | p | Mute time inputs are always built against the main window | Create the fields with the window that asked (the Asker's window) and key them to it. |
| 11-p5-016 | `src/ui/notification_settings/scope.rs:13` | bug | p | Scope/reaction notification settings are sent as a whole object copied from the session cache, without an optimistic update | Keep a pending patch per scope (request id + the fields changed) and rebase on it, or send only the changed field when TDLib allows. |

| 11-p7-005 | `src/ui/passcode/mod.rs:373` | bug | p | Passcode job result is thrown away when its window closed | Run the result handler on the main window when the asking window is gone (as unlock_in_main_window does) so the answer always lands once; store the job as PasscodeJob::Running{kind, asker}. |
| 11-p8-012 | `src/ui/peer_qr/gifts_panel.rs:157` | bug | p | Gifts grid can ask for the next page on every frame | Set a retry deadline (or Fetch::Failed) in the early-Err path of fetch_share_theme_gifts, and make ThemeGifts one enum. |

| 13-049 | `src/ui/shared_media_select.rs:221` | bug | p | Gallery row menu acts on the gallery chat at click time, not the chat the menu was built for | Capture an AccountChat/ChatMessage owner key in the menu closure and drop the action when it no longer equals the open gallery chat. |
| 05-010 | `src/ui/spellcheck_ui.rs:327` | bug | p | recheck_spelling re-asks the backend on the UI thread for every underlined word after the cache was cleared | Run the retain on the background executor or only compare against app_words/ignored on the UI thread (no backend call). |
| 13-041 | `src/ui/spoiler_fx.rs:198` | bug | p | Media spoiler tile (60 frames of 3000 specks) is built inside paint on the UI thread and rebuilt after every 3 s idle release | Build off-thread into a Loading/Ready tile, and release only under memory pressure. |
| 05-034 | `src/ui/start_files_ui.rs:134` | bug | p | Files chosen for forwarding are taken from the chooser before the chat is known to have opened | Take the files only after open_chat_id() == Some(chat); otherwise keep the chooser open. |
| 13-036 | `src/ui/start_files_ui.rs:134` | bug | p | Files picked for a chat are dropped when that chat does not open synchronously | Keep the files until the chat is open (Choosing -> Attaching{chat}), attach on open, restore on failure. |
| 13-039 | `src/ui/startup_failure/device_loss.rs:159` | bug | p | Recover waits on request_windowing with no way out | Give the await an exit (platform error, quit-asked, or a manual-clock deadline) that ends in give_up. |
| 13-027 | `src/ui/statistics_export.rs:52` | bug | p | Export writes a workbook that silently lacks graphs that are still loading or failed, and then reports success | Refuse (or warn) while any async graph of the page is Loading or Failed. |

| 13-123 | `src/ui/story_albums/cover_strip.rs:492` | bug | p | Cover Save and Privacy Save and composer Save-edit share the single StoryManageState slot; the first call's answer is dropped when another starts | key the in-flight management call by purpose (Cover\|Privacy\|Edit) per story, and let each panel hold its own RequestId instead of a sent bool matched to a global pending |
| 13-129 | `src/ui/story_albums/mod.rs:181` | bug | p | Create album has no in-flight guard and does not clear the name; a second press creates a duplicate album | disable story-page mutations while page_op is Sending for the page and clear new_album_name on success |
| 13-132 | `src/ui/story_albums/mod.rs:475` | bug | p | Pin/unpin builds the full pinned list from the cached state and sends it without waiting for the previous answer | disable pin toggles while a pin op is Sending, or queue and rebuild from the settled list |

| 13-163 | `src/ui/translate_ui/mod.rs:141` | bug | p | One-slot offer cache thrashes when two windows show different chats | Key the cache by chat (HashMap<ChatId, ..>) and include `automatic`; drop entries for chats no window shows (as pump does). |
| 13-162 | `src/ui/translate_ui/mod.rs:421` | bug | p | A failed selection translation leaves the box on the loading placeholder | Put the job into the dialog stage as Requested(Result<job, error>) and draw Failed for an Err instead of treating no translation as pending. |
| 14-023 | `src/ui/wallpaper_cache.rs:174` | bug | p | If the wallpaper worker thread panics, every wallpaper area asks for an animation frame forever | Make the worker's liveness a value (Worker::Running/Dead) set when the guarded body returns, and fall back to inline rendering or a plain fill (no further frame requests) when it is dead. |

## S4: low impact (592)

| id | where | kind | conf | title | fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 01-016 | `src/agent/tools/people.rs:132` | bug | c | copy_new leaves an empty or partial file at the destination when the copy fails | Copy to a temp name in the destination folder and rename it into place, removing it on any error. |
| 01-018 | `src/agent/tools/td_call.rs:286` | bug | c | td_call asks the user to Allow a send that is then refused for a reason known beforehand | Run grants.writable(first_chat) before ask_user for methods in SENDS. |
| 01-032 | `src/agent/tools/ui.rs:327` | bug | c | The composer-clear command after an agent UI send is fire and forget | Hand the send and its clear over as one UiSend stage (Sent) that the UI acknowledges, or retry the post and report failure in the tool answer. |
| 01-034 | `src/agent/tools/ui_shot.rs:83` | bug | c | A screenshot is reserved against the rate limits before the command runs and not given back when the command fails | Reserve only when the UI reports it will take the picture, or release the reservation on Err/Refused. |
| 01-038 | `src/agent/tools/write/change.rs:17` | bug | c | Every error from getMessage is reported as 'There is no message'; and a failed re-read after a successful edit reports the edit as failed | Separate NotFound from other TdError; after a successful edit fall back to the request data. |
| 01-058 | `src/agent/ui_driver/shot.rs:148` | bug | c | Screenshot rate-limit slot is spent before the screenshot is taken | Reserve when the capture is about to run (or release the slot on refusal) instead of at the tool entry. |
| 02-080 | `src/connect/auth.rs:463` | bug | c | 'Code sent to' marker is cleared before the in-flight guard can refuse the request | Clear the marker in the answer/ack arm (or in a Sending stage) only after the request was accepted. |
| 02-081 | `src/connect/auth.rs:561` | bug | c | Password sign-in, recovery request and recovery code submit have no in-flight guard | Route all three through login_request (one request per purpose) and set recovery_mode only on the answer. |
| 02-084 | `src/connect/bots.rs:104` | bug | c | A group bot user whose getUser send failed is never asked again | Insert into asked only after a successful send, or keep a Fetch<()> per user. |
| 02-014 | `src/connect/call_screen.rs:511` | bug | c | Failed screen-pause toggle is never retried and shows a generic voice chat error | Own purpose for the pause toggle whose error arm reverts the flag, or resend while server and local state differ. |
| 02-016 | `src/connect/calls/history.rs:132` | bug | c | send_call_log has no in-flight guard and does blocking file IO on the UI thread; stale log file left | CallLog stage enum (Idle, Sending(RequestId), Sent, Failed) checked before the write. |
| 02-039 | `src/connect/core/mod.rs:979` | bug | c | maybe_send_parameters leaves the SetParameters pending entry when send_json fails | take(extra) on send failure like the other senders; derive 'sent' from the auth stage instead of a bool. |
| 02-053 | `src/connect/emoji_sets.rs:314` | bug | c | One shared emoji.failed bool is cleared by any emoji_set_request, hiding an earlier failure | Per-action Fetch<()> instead of a shared failed flag. |
| 02-071 | `src/connect/grams.rs:91` | bug | c | grams_tick records asked_users/asked_chats even when the lookup send failed | Insert into the asked set only when the send succeeded. |
| 02-117 | `src/connect/group_calls/live_story.rs:337` | bug | c | Deleting live story comments removes them locally at once with no restore when deleteGroupCallMessages errors | Mark messages Deleting(request) and drop them on ok, restore on error. |
| 02-110 | `src/connect/group_calls/rtmp_start.rs:54` | bug | c | Start Streaming closes the RTMP box as soon as createVideoChat is sent, not when it succeeds | RtmpStart::Starting(request) closed on the ok answer, reopened with the error on failure. |
| 02-124 | `src/connect/groups/boosts.rs:247` | bug | c | A second unrestrict-boost-count change while one is in flight is dropped with Ok(None) | Queue the latest value and send it when the first answers, or disable the control while pending. |

| 14-018 | `src/ui/video_player/controls/mod.rs:110` | bug | p | clear_clip leaves speed_preview set, so a viewer whose speed drag was cut off keeps its controls up and shows a stale speed | One gesture value: enum ControlsDrag { None, Seek(f64), Volume, Speed(f64) }, reset whole by clear_clip. |
| 14-028 | `src/ui/video_player/mod.rs:792` | bug | p | A file player stuck in Opening asks for a display frame every vsync with no limit | Bound the Opening wait too (an open deadline that fails the player), or let the 50 ms open poll drive redraws instead of per-vsync frame requests. |
| 14-024 | `src/ui/wallpaper_cache.rs:51` | bug | p | Slot::Preview is one slot for every preview area in every window, so two previews replace each other's render forever | Hash the window id (and the owning dialog) into the preview slot as History does, or give each preview area its own slot key. |
| 14-026 | `src/ui/wallpaper_source.rs:93` | bug | p | A pattern or photo that failed to read is remembered as None, and its fill-only result is cached under the same key | Do not remember failures (or remember them with a retry time), and do not cache a degraded render under the final key. |
| 14-035 | `src/ui/web_app_ui/emoji_status.rs:165` | bug | p | Emoji status access stuck in Granting when the toggle answer never comes | Give Granting the request id and a deadline (as PhoneFlow and SetRequest have) and leave it on a dropped request. |
| 14-040 | `src/ui/web_app_ui/mod.rs:279` | bug | p | Mini app render_action is one slot; a second action before the next render drops the first | A queue (Vec<RenderAction>) drained at render, or answer 'failed' to an invoice action that is replaced. |
| 14-043 | `src/ui/web_app_ui/spawn_mini_app_window.rs:170` | bug | p | Mini app from_window is one per screen and overwritten by every later flow | Keep the asking window inside each pending flow value (PendingOpen/LaunchFlow) instead of one shared field. |
| 14-041 | `src/ui/web_app_ui/spawn_mini_app_window.rs:233` | bug | p | Mini app open answers are applied in arrival order, not to the launch the user asked for last | Keep only the latest asked launch as current (drop older pending launches and close their answers), and make open_result a queue or keyed by request. |
| 14-042 | `src/ui/web_app_ui/spawn_mini_app_window.rs:322` | bug | p | awaiting_toggle continues on any attachment-menu toggle answer, not the one it waits for | Carry the bot id (or request id) in awaiting_toggle and match the toggled answer to it. |
| 14-046 | `src/ui/widget_bubble.rs:181` | bug | p | A widget press or hover sticks when its bubble stops painting mid-gesture | Release the press/hover when the widget is not drawn in a pass (the per-window seen pass already exists) or on window blur. |
| 14-047 | `src/ui/widget_runtime/canvas.rs:178` | crash | p | Canvases::read multiplies widget-supplied w*h*4 unchecked | checked_mul for w*h*4 and cap w*h at MAX_PIXELS; cap put_image's w*h the same way. |
| 14-051 | `src/ui/widgets_state.rs:66` | bug | p | WidgetEntry::pressed sticks when the release never reaches the widget, turning later hovers into drags | Hold the press in the live runtime state (`Live::press: Option<Press>`) so a stop or restart drops it, clear it in hide_widgets, and end it on a window-level mouse-up rather than the layer's. |
| 14-058 | `src/ui/widgets_ui.rs:261` | bug | p | New widget content keeps the old content's frame and height, so a new runtime that fails shows the old picture as 'stopped' | On an identity change replace the entry's content-bound part whole (frame, height from the stored value, poll, live runtime), e.g. `entry.content = ContentState::new(identity)`. |
| 14-054 | `src/ui/widgets_ui.rs:587` | bug | p | Widget query tokens restart with every OnScreen while their answers queue in the account's Session, so an old answer can match a new query | Make query tokens process-wide (a static counter) or tag them with the ScreenEpoch, so a token is never issued twice for one account session. |
| 14-057 | `src/ui/widgets_ui.rs:726` | bug | p | A public data poll that is never answered leaves the widget in Poll::Asking for good | Give Asking a deadline (`Asking { since: Instant }`) after which it returns to Due, or tie it to the request id the driver returns so a dropped request ends it. |
| 14-060 | `src/ui/window_control.rs:258` | bug | p | The X11 connection is cached for the process even after it breaks | Drop the cached connection when a request on it fails (`Option` replaced by `Connected \| Broken`), so the next call reconnects. |
| 14-068 | `src/update_install.rs:701` | bug | p | The update helper gives up after 30 s of waiting for the app to exit and starts the old Quill again while the first one is still quitting | Wait on something only the parent holds (the claim's lock, already shared with the helper) instead of the pid, with no fixed deadline while that lock is held. |
| 14-061 | `src/video_decode/mod.rs:843` | bug | p | Player::seek clears `eof` but not `error`, so a clip that hit a decode error keeps reporting it after a seek that works | Hold the decode stage as one value: `enum Decoding { Running, Ended, Failed(String), Stopped }` that a seek replaces whole (Failed -> Running when the seek is accepted). |
| 14-077 | `src/web_app/bridge.rs:341` | bug | p | Bridge req_ids are cut to 64 chars, so the reply carries a req_id the page never sent | Refuse an over-long req_id outright (BadArguments with a failure reply) instead of truncating it, so the answer always carries the id that asked. |
| 14-078 | `src/web_app/bridge.rs:495` | bug | p | A refused popup or custom-method request gets no reply event, so the page waits forever | Make bad arguments of request-style events produce the bridge's failure reply (popup_closed with no button, custom_method_invoked with an error and the req_id) like `refusal` does for unsupported ones. |
| 14-080 | `src/webauthn/ceremony.rs:414` | bug | p | The PIN asked for one key is tried on every plugged-in key in turn | Carry the key that asked for the PIN (device path/index) with the PIN and try it only on that key in the next attempt. |
| 14-079 | `src/webauthn/ctap2/hid_linux.rs:202` | bug | p | A security key Quill may not open (no udev rule) reads as 'no key' after the whole timeout | Return the open error from open_all (an enum Found { Keys(..), Denied(path) }) and end the ceremony with a permission error the dialog can show. |

| id | where | kind | conf | title | fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 01-001 | `crates/webview-host/src/carrier/mod.rs:323` | implicit-state | c | Carrier helper phase is spread over armed AtomicBool, view Option, relay.closing_at Option and wait_for_browser bool | One `enum Phase { LockingDown, Loading, Closing{at,wait_for_browser}, Gone }` owned by the loop; the flag the web view callbacks read becomes a copy of it. |
